### Comment by [@michael-pryor](https://github.com/michael-pryor) — 2026-06-04T12:00:29Z

We run Kueue at scale managing GPU workloads across multiple clusters, and manually keeping `nominalQuota` in sync with actual node capacity is one of our biggest operational pain points — especially during rolling upgrades when nodes move between clusters and capacity fluctuates. The `NodeQuotaPolicy` approach proposed here is exactly what we need: it cleanly separates the "what is the quota" from "how is it derived", and the headroom/resourceOverrides flexibility covers our real-world requirements well. We'd be happy to help test an Alpha implementation against our setup.

+# KEP-10270: Automatic ClusterQueue quota limits based on node selectors
+

+  - [Non-Goals](#non-goals)

+- [Design Details](#design-details)
+  - [API Changes](#api-changes)
+    - [ClusterQueue Spec](#clusterqueue-spec)
+    - [Validation rules](#validation-rules)
+    - [ClusterQueue Status Extension](#clusterqueue-status-extension)
+  - [Controller Design](#controller-design)
+    - [Node watcher](#node-watcher)
+    - [Quota calculation](#quota-calculation)
+    - [Reconciliation loop](#reconciliation-loop)
+  - [Interaction with Existing Features](#interaction-with-existing-features)
+  - [RBAC Requirements](#rbac-requirements)
+  - [Test Plan](#test-plan)
+    - [Prerequisite testing updates](#prerequisite-testing-updates)

+    - [e2e tests](#e2e-tests)
+  - [Graduation Criteria](#graduation-criteria)
+    - [Alpha](#alpha)
+    - [Beta](#beta)
+    - [GA](#ga)
+- [Implementation History](#implementation-history)
+<!-- /toc -->
+
+## Summary
+
+This KEP proposes extending the ClusterQueue API to support automatic quota
+detection based on node selectors. Instead of requiring administrators to
+manually set and maintain `nominalQuota` values, Kueue can dynamically compute
+quotas by watching Kubernetes Nodes that match a given selector and aggregating
+their Allocatable resources. This removes the operational burden of keeping
+ClusterQueue quotas in sync with the underlying infrastructure, especially in
+autoscaled clusters where node pools frequently change size.
+
+## Motivation
+
+Currently, `nominalQuota` in a ClusterQueue ResourceQuota is a static value that
+administrators must manually configure and update. When the underlying node pool
+scales up or down (e.g., via cluster-autoscaler or manually), the administrator
+must manually adjust the ClusterQueue quota to reflect the new cluster capacity.
+This creates several problems:
+
+1. **Operational overhead**: In dynamic environments, node pools scale
+   frequently. Manually updating ClusterQueue quotas for each scaling event is
+   error-prone and labor intensive.
+2. **Quota drift**: If quotas are not updated promptly after a scaling event,
+   Kueue may over-admit workloads (quota > actual capacity) or under-utilize the
+   cluster (quota < actual capacity).
+3. **Redundant configuration**: Capacity management is already handled at the
+   node pool level (e.g. via max node count settings in cloud providers).
+   Requiring administrators to duplicate this information in ClusterQueue
+   configuration is redundant.
+4. **ResourceFlavor already knows the nodes**: A ResourceFlavor specifies
+   `nodeLabels` that associate it with a set of Nodes. However, Kueue does not use
+   this information to compute how many resources those nodes actually provide.
+   The administrator must separately and manually state the total quota.
+
+### Goals
+
+- Allow ClusterQueue quotas to be automatically computed from the Allocatable
+  resources of Nodes matching a selector.
+- Support configurable headroom to reserve resources for system and non-Kueue
+  workloads.
+- Ensure automatic quotas update dynamically as Nodes are added, removed, or
+  change status.
+- Be backward compatible — existing static `nominalQuota` configurations
+  continue to work unchanged.
+- Support coexistence of auto-detected and manually specified quotas within the
+  same ClusterQueue.
+
+### Non-Goals
+
+- Replace or deprecate static `nominalQuota`. Both modes will coexist.
+- Automatically create or manage ResourceFlavors.
+- Account for resources consumed by non-Kueue workloads (DaemonSets, system pods)
+  beyond a configurable headroom percentage or fixed amount.
+- Integrate with cluster-autoscaler to predict future capacity. This feature
+  reflects current capacity only. Future capacity planning is covered by
+  ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- Introduce auto-detection for DRA Device resources. DRA quota is managed through
+  DeviceClassMappings (KEP-2941).
+
+## Proposal
+
+Add an optional `autoDetect` field to ResourceQuota within the ClusterQueue spec.
+When `autoDetect` is set, Kueue watches Nodes matching the specified selector,
+aggregates their Allocatable values for the resource, subtracts the configured
+headroom, and uses the result as the effective `nominalQuota`. The static
+`nominalQuota` field becomes optional when `autoDetect` is specified.
+
+### User Stories
+
+#### Story 1
+
+As a cluster administrator, I manage a GPU node pool that autos-scales between 2
+and 20 nodes. Each node has 8 GPUs. I want Kueue to automatically know that the
+cluster has between 16 and 160 GPUs available, without me updating the ClusterQueue
+every time a node is added or removed.
+
+```yaml
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: gpu-team-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["nvidia.com/gpu"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: gpu-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: nvidia.com/gpu
+        autoDetect:
+          nodeSelector:
```

  I would simplify the API to just be node selectors:
  ```
  apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
  kind: ClusterQueue
  metadata:
    name: gpu-team-cq
  spec:
    resourceGroups:
    - coveredResources: ["nvidia.com/gpu"]
      flavors:
      - name: gpu-nodes
        resources:
        - name: nvidia.com/gpu
          nodeSelector:
            matchLabels:
              gpu-type: a100
  ```
  
  But there needs to be validation such that it's mutually exclusive with inline quotas

+  ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- Introduce auto-detection for DRA Device resources. DRA quota is managed through
+  DeviceClassMappings (KEP-2941).
+
+## Proposal
+
+Add an optional `autoDetect` field to ResourceQuota within the ClusterQueue spec.

+
+As a cluster administrator, I manage a GPU node pool that autos-scales between 2

+
+```yaml
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2

+  name: gpu-team-cq

+      - name: nvidia.com/gpu

+          nodeSelector:
+            matchLabels:
+              gpu-type: a100
+```
+
+#### Story 2
+
+As a platform engineer, I run a multi-tenant cluster where different teams use
+different node pools. I want each team's ClusterQueue to automatically reflect
+the capacity of their dedicated node pool, with 5% headroom reserved for system
+DaemonSets.
+
+```yaml
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: team-alpha-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["cpu", "memory"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: team-alpha-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: cpu
+        autoDetect:
+          nodeSelector:
+            matchLabels:
+              team: alpha
+          headroom:
+            percentage: 5
```

  can the headroom just be inherited from kubelet system resources? https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/administer-cluster/reserve-compute-resources/

+
+## Proposal
+
+Add an optional `autoDetect` field to ResourceQuota within the ClusterQueue spec.
+When `autoDetect` is set, Kueue watches Nodes matching the specified selector,
+aggregates their Allocatable values for the resource, subtracts the configured
+headroom, and uses the result as the effective `nominalQuota`. The static
+`nominalQuota` field becomes optional when `autoDetect` is specified.
+
+### User Stories
+
+#### Story 1
+
+As a cluster administrator, I manage a GPU node pool that autos-scales between 2
+and 20 nodes. Each node has 8 GPUs. I want Kueue to automatically know that the
+cluster has between 16 and 160 GPUs available, without me updating the ClusterQueue
+every time a node is added or removed.
+
+```yaml
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: gpu-team-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["nvidia.com/gpu"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: gpu-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: nvidia.com/gpu
+        autoDetect:
+          nodeSelector:
```

@@ -0,0 +1,466 @@
+# KEP-10270: Automatic ClusterQueue quota limits based on node selectors
+
+<!-- toc -->
+- [Summary](#summary)
+- [Motivation](#motivation)
+  - [Goals](#goals)
+  - [Non-Goals](#non-goals)
+- [Proposal](#proposal)
+  - [User Stories (Optional)](#user-stories-optional)
+    - [Story 1](#story-1)
+    - [Story 2](#story-2)
+    - [Story 3](#story-3)
+  - [Notes/Constraints/Caveats (Optional)](#notesconstraintscaveats-optional)
+  - [Risks and Mitigations](#risks-and-mitigations)
+- [Design Details](#design-details)
+  - [API Changes](#api-changes)
+    - [ClusterQueue Spec](#clusterqueue-spec)
+    - [Validation rules](#validation-rules)
+    - [ClusterQueue Status Extension](#clusterqueue-status-extension)
+  - [Controller Design](#controller-design)
+    - [Node watcher](#node-watcher)
+    - [Quota calculation](#quota-calculation)
+    - [Reconciliation loop](#reconciliation-loop)
+  - [Interaction with Existing Features](#interaction-with-existing-features)
+  - [RBAC Requirements](#rbac-requirements)
+  - [Test Plan](#test-plan)
+    - [Prerequisite testing updates](#prerequisite-testing-updates)
+    - [Unit tests](#unit-tests)
+    - [Integration tests](#integration-tests)
+    - [e2e tests](#e2e-tests)
+  - [Graduation Criteria](#graduation-criteria)
+    - [Alpha](#alpha)
+    - [Beta](#beta)
+    - [GA](#ga)
+- [Implementation History](#implementation-history)
+<!-- /toc -->
+
+## Summary
+
+This KEP proposes extending the ClusterQueue API to support automatic quota
+detection based on node selectors. Instead of requiring administrators to
+manually set and maintain `nominalQuota` values, Kueue can dynamically compute
+quotas by watching Kubernetes Nodes that match a given selector and aggregating
+their Allocatable resources. This removes the operational burden of keeping
+ClusterQueue quotas in sync with the underlying infrastructure, especially in
+autoscaled clusters where node pools frequently change size.
+
+## Motivation
+
+Currently, `nominalQuota` in a ClusterQueue ResourceQuota is a static value that
+administrators must manually configure and update. When the underlying node pool
+scales up or down (e.g., via cluster-autoscaler or manually), the administrator
+must manually adjust the ClusterQueue quota to reflect the new cluster capacity.
+This creates several problems:
+
+1. **Operational overhead**: In dynamic environments, node pools scale
+   frequently. Manually updating ClusterQueue quotas for each scaling event is
+   error-prone and labor intensive.
+2. **Quota drift**: If quotas are not updated promptly after a scaling event,
+   Kueue may over-admit workloads (quota > actual capacity) or under-utilize the
+   cluster (quota < actual capacity).
+3. **Redundant configuration**: Capacity management is already handled at the
+   node pool level (e.g. via max node count settings in cloud providers).
+   Requiring administrators to duplicate this information in ClusterQueue
+   configuration is redundant.
+4. **ResourceFlavor already knows the nodes**: A ResourceFlavor specifies
+   `nodeLabels` that associate it with a set of Nodes. However, Kueue does not use
+   this information to compute how many resources those nodes actually provide.
+   The administrator must separately and manually state the total quota.
+
+### Goals
+
+- Allow ClusterQueue quotas to be automatically computed from the Allocatable
+  resources of Nodes matching a selector.
+- Support configurable headroom to reserve resources for system and non-Kueue
+  workloads.
+- Ensure automatic quotas update dynamically as Nodes are added, removed, or
+  change status.
+- Be backward compatible — existing static `nominalQuota` configurations
+  continue to work unchanged.
+- Support coexistence of auto-detected and manually specified quotas within the
+  same ClusterQueue.
+
+### Non-Goals
+
+- Replace or deprecate static `nominalQuota`. Both modes will coexist.
+- Automatically create or manage ResourceFlavors.
+- Account for resources consumed by non-Kueue workloads (DaemonSets, system pods)
+  beyond a configurable headroom percentage or fixed amount.
+- Integrate with cluster-autoscaler to predict future capacity. This feature
+  reflects current capacity only. Future capacity planning is covered by
+  ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- Introduce auto-detection for DRA Device resources. DRA quota is managed through
+  DeviceClassMappings (KEP-2941).
+
+## Proposal
+
+Add an optional `autoDetect` field to ResourceQuota within the ClusterQueue spec.
+When `autoDetect` is set, Kueue watches Nodes matching the specified selector,
+aggregates their Allocatable values for the resource, subtracts the configured
+headroom, and uses the result as the effective `nominalQuota`. The static
+`nominalQuota` field becomes optional when `autoDetect` is specified.
+
+### User Stories
+
+#### Story 1
+
+As a cluster administrator, I manage a GPU node pool that autos-scales between 2
+and 20 nodes. Each node has 8 GPUs. I want Kueue to automatically know that the
+cluster has between 16 and 160 GPUs available, without me updating the ClusterQueue
+every time a node is added or removed.
+
+```yaml
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: gpu-team-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["nvidia.com/gpu"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: gpu-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: nvidia.com/gpu
+        autoDetect:
+          nodeSelector:
+            matchLabels:
+              gpu-type: a100
+```
+
+#### Story 2
+
+As a platform engineer, I run a multi-tenant cluster where different teams use
+different node pools. I want each team's ClusterQueue to automatically reflect
+the capacity of their dedicated node pool, with 5% headroom reserved for system
+DaemonSets.
+
+```yaml
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: team-alpha-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["cpu", "memory"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: team-alpha-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: cpu
+        autoDetect:
+          nodeSelector:
+            matchLabels:
+              team: alpha
+          headroom:
+            percentage: 5
+      - name: memory
+        autoDetect:
+          nodeSelector:
+            matchLabels:
+              team: alpha
+          headroom:
+            percentage: 5
+```
+
+#### Story 3
+
+As an administrator, I want to use auto-detection for standard resources (cpu,
+memory) but keep manual quota for a custom resource that is not reported by the
+node.
+
+```yaml
+resources:
+- name: cpu
+  autoDetect:
+    nodeSelector:
+      matchLabels:
+        node-pool: main
+- name: memory
+  autoDetect:
+    nodeSelector:
+      matchLabels:
+        node-pool: main
+- name: example.com/special-token
+  nominalQuota: 100 # manual, no auto-detection
+```
+
+### Notes/Constraints/Caveats
+
+- `autoDetect` and `nominalQuota` are mutually exclusive on the same resource
+  entry. Setting both is a validation error. This avoids ambiguity about which
+  value takes precedence.
+- Node readiness filtering: By default only Nodes with condition Ready=True are
+  counted. An optional field `includeNotReady` can override this, but it defaults
+  to false. This prevents quota inflation due to broken nodes.
+- Interaction with ResourceFlavor nodeLabels: The auto-detect nodeSelector is
```

  Does it make sense for quotas to just reference ResourceFlavors for "auto detection" (which reference node selectors)? ClusterQueues already reference ResourceFlavors so this would be an even simpler API. Is there a reason we can't do that?

```

  Took a look. I don't think you should do this at the CQ level. I think you should lift this up to the cohort level. 
  
  For large organizations doing capacity admin, if you want this automation you want the capacity to have a neutral landing place before the cluster admin can divvy it up into nominalQuota on ClusterQueues for more static upsizing. (ie you're doing node onboarding once a month) I think it makes sense for node autoscaling too.

```

  I don't know... if this is how Kueue's interaction with node autoscaling works. So I think the flow for Kueue normally for node autoscaling is this:
  - You define a CQ with 16GPU, real cluster capacity is 8GPUs
  - You schedule a 16GPU workload. Kueue makes a ProvisioningRequest that tells the autoscaler to go provision those nodes.
  
  So if you do the automatic node detection to adjust nominalQuota based off label, I think it would doublecount. It seems like youre doing node autoscaling out of band from Kueue.

+- Integrate with cluster-autoscaler to predict future capacity. This feature
+  reflects current capacity only. Future capacity planning is covered by
+  ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).

+
+Add an optional `autoDetect` field to ResourceQuota within the ClusterQueue spec.
+When `autoDetect` is set, Kueue watches Nodes matching the specified selector,
+aggregates their Allocatable values for the resource, subtracts the configured
+headroom, and uses the result as the effective `nominalQuota`. The static
+`nominalQuota` field becomes optional when `autoDetect` is specified.

+

+    - [Alpha](#alpha)
+    - [Beta](#beta)
+    - [GA](#ga)
+- [Implementation History](#implementation-history)
+<!-- /toc -->
+
+## Summary
+
+This KEP proposes extending the ClusterQueue API to support automatic quota
+detection based on node selectors. Instead of requiring administrators to
+manually set and maintain `nominalQuota` values, Kueue can dynamically compute
+quotas by watching Kubernetes Nodes that match a given selector and aggregating
+their Allocatable resources. This removes the operational burden of keeping
+ClusterQueue quotas in sync with the underlying infrastructure, especially in
+autoscaled clusters where node pools frequently change size.
+
+## Motivation
+
+Currently, `nominalQuota` in a ClusterQueue ResourceQuota is a static value that
+administrators must manually configure and update. When the underlying node pool
+scales up or down (e.g., via cluster-autoscaler or manually), the administrator
+must manually adjust the ClusterQueue quota to reflect the new cluster capacity.
+This creates several problems:
+
+1. **Operational overhead**: In dynamic environments, node pools scale
+   frequently. Manually updating ClusterQueue quotas for each scaling event is
+   error-prone and labor intensive.
+2. **Quota drift**: If quotas are not updated promptly after a scaling event,
+   Kueue may over-admit workloads (quota > actual capacity) or under-utilize the
+   cluster (quota < actual capacity).
+3. **Redundant configuration**: Capacity management is already handled at the
+   node pool level (e.g. via max node count settings in cloud providers).
+   Requiring administrators to duplicate this information in ClusterQueue
+   configuration is redundant.
+4. **ResourceFlavor already knows the nodes**: A ResourceFlavor specifies
+   `nodeLabels` that associate it with a set of Nodes. However, Kueue does not use
+   this information to compute how many resources those nodes actually provide.
+   The administrator must separately and manually state the total quota.
+
+### Goals
+
+- Allow ClusterQueue quotas to be automatically computed from the Allocatable
+  resources of Nodes matching a selector.
+- Support configurable headroom to reserve resources for system and non-Kueue
+  workloads.
+- Ensure automatic quotas update dynamically as Nodes are added, removed, or
+  change status.
+- Be backward compatible — existing static `nominalQuota` configurations
+  continue to work unchanged.
+- Support coexistence of auto-detected and manually specified quotas within the
+  same ClusterQueue.
+
+### Non-Goals
+
+- Replace or deprecate static `nominalQuota`. Both modes will coexist.
+- Automatically create or manage ResourceFlavors.
+- Account for resources consumed by non-Kueue workloads (DaemonSets, system pods)
+  beyond a configurable headroom percentage or fixed amount.
+- Integrate with cluster-autoscaler to predict future capacity. This feature
+  reflects current capacity only. Future capacity planning is covered by
+  ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- Introduce auto-detection for DRA Device resources. DRA quota is managed through
+  DeviceClassMappings (KEP-2941).
+
+## Proposal
+
+Add an optional `autoDetect` field to ResourceQuota within the ClusterQueue spec.
+When `autoDetect` is set, Kueue watches Nodes matching the specified selector,
+aggregates their Allocatable values for the resource, subtracts the configured
+headroom, and uses the result as the effective `nominalQuota`. The static
+`nominalQuota` field becomes optional when `autoDetect` is specified.
+
+### User Stories
+
+#### Story 1
+
+As a cluster administrator, I manage a GPU node pool that autos-scales between 2
+and 20 nodes. Each node has 8 GPUs. I want Kueue to automatically know that the
+cluster has between 16 and 160 GPUs available, without me updating the ClusterQueue
+every time a node is added or removed.
+
+```yaml
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: gpu-team-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["nvidia.com/gpu"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: gpu-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: nvidia.com/gpu
+        autoDetect:
+          nodeSelector:
+            matchLabels:
+              gpu-type: a100
+```
+
+#### Story 2
+
+As a platform engineer, I run a multi-tenant cluster where different teams use
+different node pools. I want each team's ClusterQueue to automatically reflect
+the capacity of their dedicated node pool, with 5% headroom reserved for system
+DaemonSets.
+
+```yaml
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: team-alpha-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["cpu", "memory"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: team-alpha-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: cpu
+        autoDetect:
+          nodeSelector:
+            matchLabels:
+              team: alpha
+          headroom:
+            percentage: 5
```

  Is this related to the headroom proposal from Karpenter?

```

  I have doubts that this thing belongs to CQ mechanics. CQ should not be interested in **HOW** quotas are established. From CQ point of view sysadmin manually adjusting quotas up to their taste, automatic quota propagation from some company tools  budget planning or inventory manager, or automatic quota configuration based on node selector is all the same.
  
  On the other hand I can see a need for quota reconfiguration based on some criteria. Existing capacity is a very solid signal, we should have it, but I have a feeling its configuration and update mechanics should be in a different API object and controllers.

+   The administrator must separately and manually state the total quota.
+
+### Goals
+
+- Allow ClusterQueue quotas to be automatically computed from the Allocatable
+  resources of Nodes matching a selector.
+- Support configurable headroom to reserve resources for system and non-Kueue
+  workloads.
+- Ensure automatic quotas update dynamically as Nodes are added, removed, or
+  change status.
+- Be backward compatible — existing static `nominalQuota` configurations
+  continue to work unchanged.
+- Support coexistence of auto-detected and manually specified quotas within the
+  same ClusterQueue.
+
+### Non-Goals
+
+- Replace or deprecate static `nominalQuota`. Both modes will coexist.
+- Automatically create or manage ResourceFlavors.
+- Account for resources consumed by non-Kueue workloads (DaemonSets, system pods)
+  beyond a configurable headroom percentage or fixed amount.
+- Integrate with cluster-autoscaler to predict future capacity. This feature
+  reflects current capacity only. Future capacity planning is covered by
+  ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- Introduce auto-detection for DRA Device resources. DRA quota is managed through
+  DeviceClassMappings (KEP-2941).
+
+## Proposal
+
+Add an optional `autoDetect` field to ResourceQuota within the ClusterQueue spec.
+When `autoDetect` is set, Kueue watches Nodes matching the specified selector,
+aggregates their Allocatable values for the resource, subtracts the configured
+headroom, and uses the result as the effective `nominalQuota`. The static
+`nominalQuota` field becomes optional when `autoDetect` is specified.
+
+### User Stories
+
+#### Story 1
+
+As a cluster administrator, I manage a GPU node pool that autos-scales between 2
+and 20 nodes. Each node has 8 GPUs. I want Kueue to automatically know that the
+cluster has between 16 and 160 GPUs available, without me updating the ClusterQueue
+every time a node is added or removed.
+
+```yaml
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: gpu-team-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["nvidia.com/gpu"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: gpu-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: nvidia.com/gpu
+        autoDetect:
+          nodeSelector:
```

  Good suggestion. Agreed on both points:
  
  - Simplified API: Dropped the autoDetect wrapper entirely. Instead, autoDetect: true is a boolean flag at the flavor level (FlavorQuotas), not a nested struct on each resource entry. This means one flag covers all resources within a flavor, no repetition per resource.
  - Mutual exclusivity: When autoDetect: true is set on a flavor, individual resource entries must not set nominalQuota (CEL validation). The one exception is a per-resource override for resources not reported by nodes (e.g., custom tokens), this is explicitly permitted and documented.

+    - [Beta](#beta)
+    - [GA](#ga)

+
+## Summary
+
+This KEP proposes extending the ClusterQueue API to support automatic quota
+detection based on node selectors. Instead of requiring administrators to
+manually set and maintain `nominalQuota` values, Kueue can dynamically compute
+quotas by watching Kubernetes Nodes that match a given selector and aggregating
+their Allocatable resources. This removes the operational burden of keeping
+ClusterQueue quotas in sync with the underlying infrastructure, especially in
+autoscaled clusters where node pools frequently change size.
+
+## Motivation
+
+Currently, `nominalQuota` in a ClusterQueue ResourceQuota is a static value that
+administrators must manually configure and update. When the underlying node pool
+scales up or down (e.g., via cluster-autoscaler or manually), the administrator
+must manually adjust the ClusterQueue quota to reflect the new cluster capacity.
+This creates several problems:
+
+1. **Operational overhead**: In dynamic environments, node pools scale
+   frequently. Manually updating ClusterQueue quotas for each scaling event is
+   error-prone and labor intensive.
+2. **Quota drift**: If quotas are not updated promptly after a scaling event,
+   Kueue may over-admit workloads (quota > actual capacity) or under-utilize the
+   cluster (quota < actual capacity).
+3. **Redundant configuration**: Capacity management is already handled at the
+   node pool level (e.g. via max node count settings in cloud providers).
+   Requiring administrators to duplicate this information in ClusterQueue
+   configuration is redundant.
+4. **ResourceFlavor already knows the nodes**: A ResourceFlavor specifies
+   `nodeLabels` that associate it with a set of Nodes. However, Kueue does not use
+   this information to compute how many resources those nodes actually provide.
+   The administrator must separately and manually state the total quota.
+
+### Goals
+
+- Allow ClusterQueue quotas to be automatically computed from the Allocatable
+  resources of Nodes matching a selector.
+- Support configurable headroom to reserve resources for system and non-Kueue
+  workloads.
+- Ensure automatic quotas update dynamically as Nodes are added, removed, or
+  change status.
+- Be backward compatible — existing static `nominalQuota` configurations
+  continue to work unchanged.
+- Support coexistence of auto-detected and manually specified quotas within the
+  same ClusterQueue.
+
+### Non-Goals
+
+- Replace or deprecate static `nominalQuota`. Both modes will coexist.
+- Automatically create or manage ResourceFlavors.
+- Account for resources consumed by non-Kueue workloads (DaemonSets, system pods)
+  beyond a configurable headroom percentage or fixed amount.
+- Integrate with cluster-autoscaler to predict future capacity. This feature
+  reflects current capacity only. Future capacity planning is covered by
+  ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- Introduce auto-detection for DRA Device resources. DRA quota is managed through
+  DeviceClassMappings (KEP-2941).
+
+## Proposal
+
+Add an optional `autoDetect` field to ResourceQuota within the ClusterQueue spec.
+When `autoDetect` is set, Kueue watches Nodes matching the specified selector,
+aggregates their Allocatable values for the resource, subtracts the configured
+headroom, and uses the result as the effective `nominalQuota`. The static
+`nominalQuota` field becomes optional when `autoDetect` is specified.
+
+### User Stories
+
+#### Story 1
+
+As a cluster administrator, I manage a GPU node pool that autos-scales between 2
+and 20 nodes. Each node has 8 GPUs. I want Kueue to automatically know that the
+cluster has between 16 and 160 GPUs available, without me updating the ClusterQueue
+every time a node is added or removed.
+
+```yaml
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: gpu-team-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["nvidia.com/gpu"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: gpu-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: nvidia.com/gpu
+        autoDetect:
+          nodeSelector:
+            matchLabels:
+              gpu-type: a100
+```
+
+#### Story 2
+
+As a platform engineer, I run a multi-tenant cluster where different teams use
+different node pools. I want each team's ClusterQueue to automatically reflect
+the capacity of their dedicated node pool, with 5% headroom reserved for system
+DaemonSets.
+
+```yaml
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: team-alpha-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["cpu", "memory"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: team-alpha-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: cpu
+        autoDetect:
+          nodeSelector:
+            matchLabels:
+              team: alpha
+          headroom:
+            percentage: 5
```

  Worth clarifying: node.Status.Allocatable already equals total node capacity minus kubelet's kube-reserved and system-reserved, so summing Allocatable implicitly handles those reservations without any extra configuration.
  
  The headroom field in this KEP is for a different layer: DaemonSets and other non-Kueue pods that run on the nodes and consume resources beyond what kubelet reserves. Added a note in the KEP clarifying this distinction.

+## Summary
+
+This KEP proposes extending the ClusterQueue API to support automatic quota
+detection based on node selectors. Instead of requiring administrators to
+manually set and maintain `nominalQuota` values, Kueue can dynamically compute
+quotas by watching Kubernetes Nodes that match a given selector and aggregating
+their Allocatable resources. This removes the operational burden of keeping
+ClusterQueue quotas in sync with the underlying infrastructure, especially in
+autoscaled clusters where node pools frequently change size.
+
+## Motivation
+
+Currently, `nominalQuota` in a ClusterQueue ResourceQuota is a static value that
+administrators must manually configure and update. When the underlying node pool
+scales up or down (e.g., via cluster-autoscaler or manually), the administrator
+must manually adjust the ClusterQueue quota to reflect the new cluster capacity.
+This creates several problems:
+
+1. **Operational overhead**: In dynamic environments, node pools scale
+   frequently. Manually updating ClusterQueue quotas for each scaling event is
+   error-prone and labor intensive.
+2. **Quota drift**: If quotas are not updated promptly after a scaling event,
+   Kueue may over-admit workloads (quota > actual capacity) or under-utilize the
+   cluster (quota < actual capacity).
+3. **Redundant configuration**: Capacity management is already handled at the
+   node pool level (e.g. via max node count settings in cloud providers).
+   Requiring administrators to duplicate this information in ClusterQueue
+   configuration is redundant.
+4. **ResourceFlavor already knows the nodes**: A ResourceFlavor specifies
+   `nodeLabels` that associate it with a set of Nodes. However, Kueue does not use
+   this information to compute how many resources those nodes actually provide.
+   The administrator must separately and manually state the total quota.
+
+### Goals
+
+- Allow ClusterQueue quotas to be automatically computed from the Allocatable
+  resources of Nodes matching a selector.
+- Support configurable headroom to reserve resources for system and non-Kueue
+  workloads.
+- Ensure automatic quotas update dynamically as Nodes are added, removed, or
+  change status.
+- Be backward compatible — existing static `nominalQuota` configurations
+  continue to work unchanged.
+- Support coexistence of auto-detected and manually specified quotas within the
+  same ClusterQueue.
+
+### Non-Goals
+
+- Replace or deprecate static `nominalQuota`. Both modes will coexist.
+- Automatically create or manage ResourceFlavors.
+- Account for resources consumed by non-Kueue workloads (DaemonSets, system pods)
+  beyond a configurable headroom percentage or fixed amount.
+- Integrate with cluster-autoscaler to predict future capacity. This feature
+  reflects current capacity only. Future capacity planning is covered by
+  ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- Introduce auto-detection for DRA Device resources. DRA quota is managed through
+  DeviceClassMappings (KEP-2941).
+
+## Proposal
+
+Add an optional `autoDetect` field to ResourceQuota within the ClusterQueue spec.
+When `autoDetect` is set, Kueue watches Nodes matching the specified selector,
+aggregates their Allocatable values for the resource, subtracts the configured
+headroom, and uses the result as the effective `nominalQuota`. The static
+`nominalQuota` field becomes optional when `autoDetect` is specified.
+
+### User Stories
+
+#### Story 1
+
+As a cluster administrator, I manage a GPU node pool that autos-scales between 2
+and 20 nodes. Each node has 8 GPUs. I want Kueue to automatically know that the
+cluster has between 16 and 160 GPUs available, without me updating the ClusterQueue
+every time a node is added or removed.
+
+```yaml
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: gpu-team-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["nvidia.com/gpu"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: gpu-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: nvidia.com/gpu
+        autoDetect:
+          nodeSelector:
+            matchLabels:
+              gpu-type: a100
+```
+
+#### Story 2
+
+As a platform engineer, I run a multi-tenant cluster where different teams use
+different node pools. I want each team's ClusterQueue to automatically reflect
+the capacity of their dedicated node pool, with 5% headroom reserved for system
+DaemonSets.
+
+```yaml
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: team-alpha-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["cpu", "memory"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: team-alpha-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: cpu
+        autoDetect:
+          nodeSelector:
+            matchLabels:
+              team: alpha
+          headroom:
+            percentage: 5
+      - name: memory
+        autoDetect:
+          nodeSelector:
+            matchLabels:
+              team: alpha
+          headroom:
+            percentage: 5
+```
+
+#### Story 3
+
+As an administrator, I want to use auto-detection for standard resources (cpu,
+memory) but keep manual quota for a custom resource that is not reported by the
+node.
+
+```yaml
+resources:
+- name: cpu
+  autoDetect:
+    nodeSelector:
+      matchLabels:
+        node-pool: main
+- name: memory
+  autoDetect:
+    nodeSelector:
+      matchLabels:
+        node-pool: main
+- name: example.com/special-token
+  nominalQuota: 100 # manual, no auto-detection
+```
+
+### Notes/Constraints/Caveats
+
+- `autoDetect` and `nominalQuota` are mutually exclusive on the same resource
+  entry. Setting both is a validation error. This avoids ambiguity about which
+  value takes precedence.
+- Node readiness filtering: By default only Nodes with condition Ready=True are
+  counted. An optional field `includeNotReady` can override this, but it defaults
+  to false. This prevents quota inflation due to broken nodes.
+- Interaction with ResourceFlavor nodeLabels: The auto-detect nodeSelector is
```

  Yes, that's exactly the direction we went. No new nodeSelector field is introduced. The autoDetect: true flag at the flavor level tells Kueue to use the associated ResourceFlavor's nodeLabels directly for node selection — single source of truth, no duplication.
  
  For the advanced case where the same ResourceFlavor covers a broad node class but a CQ needs quota from only a subset (e.g., one region), Beta adds an optional nodeLabels field at the flavor entry level. The effective node set is then the intersection of the ResourceFlavor's nodeLabels and the flavor entry's nodeLabels. This avoids creating additional ResourceFlavors per region. See Story 4 in the updated KEP.

```

  I was going to support both: ClusterQueue and Cohort level. The updated KEP supports autoDetect: true at both the Cohort and ClusterQueue flavor levels. Since FlavorQuotas is already shared by both ClusterQueue.spec.resourceGroups and Cohort.spec.resourceGroups, no additional struct changes are needed.
  
  The pattern you described (Story 3 in the KEP):
  
  - Cohort flavor sets autoDetect: true -> capacity auto-detected from nodes lands in the cohort as a shared pool
  - Member CQs declare nominalQuota: 0 and borrow from the cohort pool
  - Admins can incrementally lock down allocations by bumping individual CQ quotas over time
  
  One constraint: a Cohort and a member CQ may both set autoDetect: true on the same flavor only if their ResourceFlavors' nodeLabels select different physical nodes. Overlapping selectors cause double-counting (Cohort nominalQuota represents additional shared resources on top of CQ quotas). Beta will add validation warnings for overlapping node sets.

+detection based on node selectors. Instead of requiring administrators to
+manually set and maintain `nominalQuota` values, Kueue can dynamically compute
+quotas by watching Kubernetes Nodes that match a given selector and aggregating
+their Allocatable resources. This removes the operational burden of keeping

+autoscaled clusters where node pools frequently change size.

+Currently, `nominalQuota` in a ClusterQueue ResourceQuota is a static value that
+administrators must manually configure and update. When the underlying node pool

+must manually adjust the ClusterQueue quota to reflect the new cluster capacity.
+This creates several problems:
+
+1. **Operational overhead**: In dynamic environments, node pools scale
+   frequently. Manually updating ClusterQueue quotas for each scaling event is
+   error-prone and labor intensive.
+2. **Quota drift**: If quotas are not updated promptly after a scaling event,

+   node pool level (e.g. via max node count settings in cloud providers).

+   configuration is redundant.

+   `nodeLabels` that associate it with a set of Nodes. However, Kueue does not use
+   this information to compute how many resources those nodes actually provide.
+   The administrator must separately and manually state the total quota.
+

+
+- Allow ClusterQueue quotas to be automatically computed from the Allocatable
+  resources of Nodes matching a selector.
+- Support configurable headroom to reserve resources for system and non-Kueue
+  workloads.
+- Ensure automatic quotas update dynamically as Nodes are added, removed, or
+  change status.
+- Be backward compatible — existing static `nominalQuota` configurations
+  continue to work unchanged.
+- Support coexistence of auto-detected and manually specified quotas within the
+  same ClusterQueue.
+
+### Non-Goals
+
+- Replace or deprecate static `nominalQuota`. Both modes will coexist.
+- Automatically create or manage ResourceFlavors.
+- Account for resources consumed by non-Kueue workloads (DaemonSets, system pods)
+  beyond a configurable headroom percentage or fixed amount.
+- Integrate with cluster-autoscaler to predict future capacity. This feature
+  reflects current capacity only. Future capacity planning is covered by
+  ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- Introduce auto-detection for DRA Device resources. DRA quota is managed through

+## Proposal

+Add an optional `autoDetect` field to ResourceQuota within the ClusterQueue spec.
+When `autoDetect` is set, Kueue watches Nodes matching the specified selector,
+aggregates their Allocatable values for the resource, subtracts the configured

```

  Valid concern, and the interaction table in the updated KEP addresses this explicitly.
  
  autoDetect and ProvisioningRequest target different scenarios and are not intended to be used simultaneously for the same resource:
  
  - autoDetect reflects current physical capacity, best suited for predictable, known node pools (fixed size, periodic onboarding cycles)
  - ProvisioningRequest is for reactive autoscaling, requesting capacity on demand for a specific workload
  
  There is no fundamental double-counting: once nodes provisioned by ProvisioningRequest come online, autoDetect naturally picks them up and increases the quota. But combining both mechanisms for the same resource simultaneously adds operational complexity. The KEP now explicitly states that for reactive autoscaling with ProvisioningRequest, static nominalQuota remains the recommended approach.

+# KEP-10270: Automatic ClusterQueue quota limits based on node selectors
+
+<!-- toc -->

+## Summary
+
+This KEP proposes extending the ClusterQueue API to support automatic quota
+detection based on node selectors. Instead of requiring administrators to
+manually set and maintain `nominalQuota` values, Kueue can dynamically compute
+quotas by watching Kubernetes Nodes that match a given selector and aggregating
+their Allocatable resources. This removes the operational burden of keeping
+ClusterQueue quotas in sync with the underlying infrastructure, especially in
+autoscaled clusters where node pools frequently change size.
+
+## Motivation
+
+Currently, `nominalQuota` in a ClusterQueue ResourceQuota is a static value that
+administrators must manually configure and update. When the underlying node pool
+scales up or down (e.g., via cluster-autoscaler or manually), the administrator
+must manually adjust the ClusterQueue quota to reflect the new cluster capacity.
+This creates several problems:
+
+1. **Operational overhead**: In dynamic environments, node pools scale
+   frequently. Manually updating ClusterQueue quotas for each scaling event is
+   error-prone and labor intensive.
+2. **Quota drift**: If quotas are not updated promptly after a scaling event,
+   Kueue may over-admit workloads (quota > actual capacity) or under-utilize the
+   cluster (quota < actual capacity).
+3. **Redundant configuration**: Capacity management is already handled at the
+   node pool level (e.g. via max node count settings in cloud providers).
+   Requiring administrators to duplicate this information in ClusterQueue
+   configuration is redundant.
+4. **ResourceFlavor already knows the nodes**: A ResourceFlavor specifies
+   `nodeLabels` that associate it with a set of Nodes. However, Kueue does not use
+   this information to compute how many resources those nodes actually provide.
+   The administrator must separately and manually state the total quota.
+
+### Goals
+
+- Allow ClusterQueue quotas to be automatically computed from the Allocatable
+  resources of Nodes matching a selector.
+- Support configurable headroom to reserve resources for system and non-Kueue
+  workloads.
+- Ensure automatic quotas update dynamically as Nodes are added, removed, or
+  change status.
+- Be backward compatible — existing static `nominalQuota` configurations
+  continue to work unchanged.
+- Support coexistence of auto-detected and manually specified quotas within the
+  same ClusterQueue.
+
+### Non-Goals
+
+- Replace or deprecate static `nominalQuota`. Both modes will coexist.
+- Automatically create or manage ResourceFlavors.
+- Account for resources consumed by non-Kueue workloads (DaemonSets, system pods)
+  beyond a configurable headroom percentage or fixed amount.
+- Integrate with cluster-autoscaler to predict future capacity. This feature
+  reflects current capacity only. Future capacity planning is covered by
+  ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- Introduce auto-detection for DRA Device resources. DRA quota is managed through
+  DeviceClassMappings (KEP-2941).
+
+## Proposal
+
+Add an optional `autoDetect` field to ResourceQuota within the ClusterQueue spec.
+When `autoDetect` is set, Kueue watches Nodes matching the specified selector,
+aggregates their Allocatable values for the resource, subtracts the configured
+headroom, and uses the result as the effective `nominalQuota`. The static
+`nominalQuota` field becomes optional when `autoDetect` is specified.
+
+### User Stories
+
+#### Story 1
+
+As a cluster administrator, I manage a GPU node pool that autos-scales between 2
```

  @kannon92 A custom admission check can approve or reject individual workloads but cannot proactively update nominalQuota on a ClusterQueue or Cohort. It only acts reactively after a scheduling attempt, it cannot prevent over-admission at the queue level or keep the scheduler's internal capacity snapshot accurate. The autoDetect approach updates quota proactively, so the scheduler always has an accurate view of available capacity before making scheduling decisions. Make sense?

  @mwielgus @tenzen-y That's a strong separation-of-concerns argument, and it aligns with how Kubernetes handles similar problems (e.g., HPA adjusting replicas on a Deployment rather than embedding scaling logic in the Deployment itself).
  
  The current design puts autoDetect: true in the FlavorQuotas spec, which does conflate "what the quota is" with "how it's derived." Your suggestion would mean keeping CQ/Cohort spec clean, just nominalQuota,  and introducing a separate object (e.g., QuotaDetector or NodePoolQuota) that references a CQ/Cohort, defines the node selection logic, and has its own controller that patches nominalQuota.
  
  The tradeoff I see:
  - Separate CRD: cleaner CQ/Cohort API, composable with other quota sources (budget tools, inventory systems), but users must manage an additional object type and the controller needs a reliable way to patch CQ/Cohort without conflicting with manual edits
  - Inline autoDetect: simpler UX for the common case (one object, one place), tighter integration with Kueue's internal scheduler cache/snapshot
  
  A close Kubernetes precedent is Node.Status.Allocatable, kubelet automatically computes available capacity inline on the Node object itself, rather than via a separate CRD. Our proposal follows the same principle: a
  ClusterQueue or Cohort representing a node pool should naturally reflect the actual capacity of that pool, just as a Node reflects its own Allocatable resources.
  
  The HPA/VPA pattern (separate CRD patching the target object) is appropriate when the adjustment logic is external business logic (e.g., "scale based on RPS"). But quota derived from physical node capacity is intrinsic to the capacity object, I think it's closer to Allocatable than to HPA, thougts?
  
  Thanks all for the quick responses, really appreciated! @andrewsykim @amy @kannon92 @mwielgus @tenzen-y

  With Allocatable, you don't have a definition **HOW** kubelet is supposed to calculate it. It is separated from the node object and goes to kubelet config and you have there things like systemReserved, kubeReserved etc (see: https://kubernetes.io/docs/reference/config-api/kubelet-config.v1beta1/ for details). 
  
  IMVHO, we may want to have it in the same way with CQs - a single number in the CQ definition ad a separate CRD api to define any automation for setting this number, if needed.

+

+- [NodeQuotaPolicy authored separately](#nodequotapolicy-authored-separately)
+<!-- /toc -->
+
+## Summary
+
+This KEP proposes a new cluster-scoped CRD, `NodeQuotaPolicy`, that enables automatic quota computation for ClusterQueues and Cohorts based on physical node capacity. For this design, a dedicated controller reads `NodeQuotaPolicy` objects, watches the Nodes they select, aggregates `node.Status.Allocatable`, and writes the computed `nominalQuota` back into the target ClusterQueue or Cohort.
+
+This design follows the Kubernetes principle of **separation of concerns**: ClusterQueue and Cohort remain responsible only for declaring what the quota is (a single numeric value), while `NodeQuotaPolicy` and its controller are responsible for how that quota is derived. The analogy is kubelet computing `node.Status.Allocatable` independently from the Node object's schema — the Node does not embed the calculation logic.
+
+The feature is delivered in two phases:
+
+- **Alpha**: A `NodeQuotaPolicy` CRD with `targetRef` (ClusterQueue or Cohort) and per-flavor rules. The controller uses the referenced ResourceFlavor's `nodeLabels` to select nodes and writes the summed `Allocatable` as `nominalQuota`.
+- **Beta**: An optional `nodeLabels` field in `FlavorNodeQuotaPolicy` allows administrators to narrow the effective node set via intersection with the ResourceFlavor's `nodeLabels`, enabling per-CQ regional or zonal quota subsetting without creating additional ResourceFlavors.
+
+## Motivation
+
+Currently, `nominalQuota` in a ClusterQueue or Cohort is a static value that administrators must manually configure and update. When the underlying node pool changes (e.g., monthly node onboarding, manual provisioning), administrators must manually adjust quotas to reflect the new cluster capacity. This creates several problems:
+
+1. **Operational overhead**: Manually updating quotas for each provisioning event is error-prone and labor intensive.
+2. **Quota drift**: If quotas are not updated promptly, Kueue may over-admit workloads (quota > actual capacity) or under-utilize the cluster (quota < actual capacity).
+3. **Redundant configuration**: Capacity management is already handled at the node pool level. Requiring administrators to duplicate this information in ClusterQueue or Cohort configuration is redundant.
+4. **ResourceFlavor already knows the nodes**: A ResourceFlavor specifies `nodeLabels` that associate it with a set of Nodes. However, Kueue does not use this information to compute how many resources those nodes actually provide. The administrator must separately and manually state the total quota.
+
+### Goals
+
+- Introduce a `NodeQuotaPolicy` CRD that declares how to derive `nominalQuota` for a target ClusterQueue or Cohort from the `Allocatable` resources of matching Nodes.
+- Keep ClusterQueue and Cohort APIs unchanged with respect to quota declaration: `nominalQuota` remains the single authoritative value consumed by the scheduler.
+- Support configurable headroom to reserve resources for DaemonSets and non-Kueue workloads running alongside Kueue workloads on the same nodes.
+- Ensure computed quotas update dynamically as Nodes are added, removed, or change status.
+- Be backward compatible — existing static `nominalQuota` configurations continue to work unchanged.
+- (Beta) Allow `nodeLabels` in `NodeQuotaPolicy` to narrow the node set beyond what the ResourceFlavor alone selects, enabling per-region or per-zone quota subsetting.
+
+### Non-Goals
+
+- Replace or deprecate static `nominalQuota`. Both modes coexist.
+- Automatically create or manage ResourceFlavors.
+- Account for resources consumed by non-Kueue workloads beyond a configurable headroom percentage or fixed amount.
+- Drive reactive autoscaling. This feature reflects current node capacity only. Reactive capacity provisioning via cluster-autoscaler is covered by ProvisioningRequest (KEP-1136).
+- Introduce auto-detection for DRA Device resources. DRA quota is managed through DeviceClassMappings (KEP-2941).
+
+## Proposal
+
+### User Stories
+
+#### Story 1
+
+As a cluster administrator, I manage a GPU node pool. Each node has 8 GPUs and is labeled `gpu-type=a100`. I already have a ResourceFlavor that uses this label. I want Kueue to automatically compute GPU quota from those nodes without me updating the ClusterQueue every time a node is added or removed.
+
+```yaml
+# Existing ResourceFlavor — unchanged
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ResourceFlavor
+metadata:
+  name: gpu-nodes
+spec:
+  nodeLabels:
+    gpu-type: a100
+
+---
+# ClusterQueue — unchanged, nominalQuota will be written by the controller
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: gpu-team-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["nvidia.com/gpu"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: gpu-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: nvidia.com/gpu
+        nominalQuota: "0"   # placeholder; NodeQuotaPolicy controller overwrites this
+
+---
+# NodeQuotaPolicy — declares how to compute nominalQuota for gpu-team-cq
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: NodeQuotaPolicy
+metadata:
+  name: gpu-team-auto-quota
+spec:
+  targetRef:
+    kind: ClusterQueue
+    name: gpu-team-cq
+  flavorPolicies:
+  - flavorName: gpu-nodes   # uses gpu-nodes ResourceFlavor nodeLabels: {gpu-type: a100}
+```
+
+#### Story 2
+
+As a platform engineer, I run a multi-tenant cluster where different teams use different node pools. I want each team's ClusterQueue to automatically reflect the capacity of their dedicated node pool, with 5% headroom reserved for system DaemonSets running on those nodes. One resource (`example.com/token`) is not reported by nodes and must be set manually.
+
+```yaml
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: NodeQuotaPolicy
+metadata:
+  name: team-alpha-auto-quota
+spec:
+  targetRef:
+    kind: ClusterQueue
+    name: team-alpha-cq
+  flavorPolicies:
+  - flavorName: team-alpha-nodes   # ResourceFlavor nodeLabels: {team: alpha}
+    headroom:
+      percentage: 5                # reserve 5% for DaemonSets
+    resourceOverrides:
+    - name: example.com/token      # not reported by nodes — fixed manual value
+      nominalQuota: "100"
+
+---
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: team-alpha-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["cpu", "memory", "example.com/token"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: team-alpha-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: cpu
+        nominalQuota: "0"          # overwritten by controller
+      - name: memory
+        nominalQuota: "0"          # overwritten by controller
+      - name: example.com/token
+        nominalQuota: "0"          # overwritten by resourceOverride in NodeQuotaPolicy
+```
+
+#### Story 3
+
+As a capacity administrator at a large organization, I onboard new node pools monthly. I want total capacity to automatically land at the Cohort level as a shared pool, so my team can then statically carve up `nominalQuota` across ClusterQueues.
+
+```yaml
+# NodeQuotaPolicy targets the Cohort directly
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: NodeQuotaPolicy
+metadata:
+  name: org-cohort-auto-quota
+spec:
+  targetRef:
+    kind: Cohort
+    name: org-cohort
+  flavorPolicies:
+  - flavorName: shared-nodes   # ResourceFlavor nodeLabels: {node-pool: shared}
+
+---
+# Cohort: nominalQuota written by controller
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: Cohort
+metadata:
+  name: org-cohort
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["cpu", "memory"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: shared-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: cpu
+        nominalQuota: "0"
+      - name: memory
+        nominalQuota: "0"
+
+---
+# CQs borrow from the cohort's auto-computed shared pool
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: team-alpha-cq
+spec:
+  cohortName: org-cohort
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["cpu", "memory"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: shared-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: cpu
+        nominalQuota: "0"     # borrows from cohort's auto-detected pool
+      - name: memory
+        nominalQuota: "0"
+```
+
+Note: the Cohort's `nominalQuota` represents additional shared resources on top of CQ quotas. A CQ and its parent Cohort may both be targeted by separate `NodeQuotaPolicy` objects as long as their ResourceFlavors' `nodeLabels` select **different** physical nodes to avoid double-counting.
+
+#### Story 4
+
+**(Beta)** As a cluster administrator managing a multi-region cluster, I have a ResourceFlavor `gpu-nodes` that covers all A100 GPU nodes across all regions. I want one ClusterQueue to automatically compute quota from only the `us-east` region's GPU nodes, and another from `us-west`, without creating separate ResourceFlavors per region.
+
+```yaml
+# ResourceFlavor covers all A100 nodes across regions
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ResourceFlavor
+metadata:
+  name: gpu-nodes
+spec:
+  nodeLabels:
+    gpu-type: a100
+
+---
+# NodeQuotaPolicy for us-east: narrows to region=us-east via nodeLabels (Beta)
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: NodeQuotaPolicy
+metadata:
+  name: us-east-gpu-quota
+spec:
+  targetRef:
+    kind: ClusterQueue
+    name: us-east-gpu-cq
+  flavorPolicies:
+  - flavorName: gpu-nodes
+    nodeLabels:
+      region: us-east        # effective nodes: gpu-type=a100 AND region=us-east
+
+---
+# NodeQuotaPolicy for us-west: narrows to region=us-west via nodeLabels (Beta)
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: NodeQuotaPolicy
+metadata:
+  name: us-west-gpu-quota
+spec:
+  targetRef:
+    kind: ClusterQueue
+    name: us-west-gpu-cq
+  flavorPolicies:
+  - flavorName: gpu-nodes
+    nodeLabels:
+      region: us-west        # effective nodes: gpu-type=a100 AND region=us-west
+
+---
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: us-east-gpu-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["nvidia.com/gpu"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: gpu-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: nvidia.com/gpu
+        nominalQuota: "0"
+
+---
+apiVersion: kueue.x-k8s.io/v1beta2
+kind: ClusterQueue
+metadata:
+  name: us-west-gpu-cq
+spec:
+  resourceGroups:
+  - coveredResources: ["nvidia.com/gpu"]
+    flavors:
+    - name: gpu-nodes
+      resources:
+      - name: nvidia.com/gpu
+        nominalQuota: "0"
+```
+
+### Notes/Constraints/Caveats
+
+- **Separation of concerns**: `NodeQuotaPolicy` owns the computation logic; ClusterQueue and Cohort own only the quota value. This mirrors the kubelet pattern where `node.Status.Allocatable` is computed externally and written into the Node object — the Node schema does not embed the computation.
+- **Headroom and Allocatable**: `node.Status.Allocatable` already equals total node capacity minus kubelet's `kube-reserved` and `system-reserved`, so those system reservations are implicitly handled. The `headroom` field is for DaemonSets and other non-Kueue pods that consume node resources beyond what kubelet reserves.
+- **Cohort + CQ NodeQuotaPolicy overlap**: A Cohort and a CQ within it may each be targeted by a `NodeQuotaPolicy` only if their respective ResourceFlavors' effective node sets (after label intersection) are **disjoint**. Overlapping node sets cause double-counting. Beta will add validation warnings for overlapping selectors.
```

  1. Why only "warnings"? What will be the behavior if the disjoint-ness constraint is violated?
  
  2. IIUC implementing such validation (regardless of producing warnings or errors) would require the validating webhook to list all NodeQuotaPolicies; likely also listing all Nodes in a cluster to verify disjointness. The latter feels heavy as the number of Nodes in a cluster can go in thousands. 
     Are we fine with making webhook logic so heavy?
     (I understand it's not really "listing" but "using informer cache". Still, AFAICS, webhooks seldom do so much. Hence asking).
